On 2026-09-02 ET, market analysis from Haruspex indicated a persistent bullish tilt, with 58% of the 717 analyzed stocks showing positive directional pressure. The market-wide average score stood at 56.9, signaling a collective lean towards bullish activity, even as specific dimensions presented varied signals.

## What the Data Shows

The strongest market signals emerged from the Short-Interest dimension, which recorded an average score of 62.1. This indicates significant market pressure potentially stemming from short covering opportunities for stocks like **OXY**, **WFC**, and **BIO**. These scores reflect observed market behavior rather than company fundamentals.

The Regulatory dimension showed the most notable upward movement, increasing by 1.7 points to an average score of 61.5. This shift suggests growing market attention and directional pressure around regulatory developments affecting key players such as **MA**, **AAPL**, and **TSLA**. Such changes often precede market adjustments based on perceived compliance or policy impacts.

Institutional directional signals also remained robust, with the Institutional dimension holding an average score of 61.2. This indicates continued market pressure associated with institutional capital flows in stocks like **CR**, **SHOP**, and **FLY**. These patterns suggest conviction among larger market participants.

Market sentiment, as measured by the Sentiment dimension, registered an average score of 60.2. This sustained bullish sentiment contributes to the overall positive directional pressure observed in companies such as **AM**, **ET**, and **KO**. Positive sentiment can fuel continued price momentum.

The sustained high level of WTI Crude Oil at $90.80 [data: 2026-09-02 close] also provides a notable context for specific sectors. Companies operating within the energy and refining space, including **MPC**, **SSL**, and **DINO**, may experience ongoing market pressure. This directional signal can be amplified by factors such as the Options-Flow dimension, which shows bullish pressure for energy-related names like **FRO** (Average Score: 53.8).

### Why is Short-Interest a leading indicator?

A high Short-Interest score often indicates that a significant portion of a stock's float is held by bearish investors. When market conditions shift or positive news emerges, these short positions can be quickly covered, creating buying pressure. This dynamic can lead to rapid upward market movement, making it a critical signal of potential directional pressure.

## Risk Factors

While bullish signals generally prevail, several dimensions present potential headwinds. The Insider-Trading dimension recorded a low average score of 34.7, indicating a lack of significant directional pressure from insider activity. This can suggest insiders see less immediate upside, or no immediate downside in their own company's stock.

Furthermore, the Concentration-Risk dimension registered a low average score of 45.2. This suggests a perceived increase in idiosyncratic risk for certain concentrated holdings, which could temper broader market enthusiasm. Investors should also observe the VIX at 15.48 [data: 2026-09-02 close], which, while not signaling immediate panic, remains above historical lows and warrants attention.

## What Would Change This View

The current thesis of prevailing bullish directional pressure would face invalidation under specific conditions. If the S&P 500 were to close below its 50-day moving average on two consecutive trading days, this would signal a significant shift in market momentum. A sustained rise in the VIX above 20, coupled with declining bullish percentages across more than 65% of analyzed stocks, would also suggest a weakening in broad market support.

Additionally, a material reversal in the Regulatory dimension, particularly a significant drop in its average score below 55, would challenge the current positive directional pressure influencing critical sectors. A sustained drop in WTI Crude Oil below $85 could also remove a key tailwind for the energy sector, shifting market dynamics for companies like **MPC** and **DINO**.

## Outlook

Haruspex data suggests that the near-term market environment is likely to remain characterized by general bullish pressure, particularly in areas influenced by Short-Interest dynamics and evolving regulatory landscapes. The strength in the Regulatory dimension indicates that stocks susceptible to policy shifts could experience continued directional adjustments.

However, the underlying data also points to areas requiring vigilance, such as the muted Insider-Trading signals and persistent Concentration-Risk. These factors highlight the importance of focused analysis on specific dimensions. Market participants should continue to monitor these nuanced signals for potential shifts in directional pressure as macro conditions and specific stock dynamics unfold.

## エネルギーセクター：強気圧力優勢

Haruspexの分析によると、エネルギーセクターは顕著な強気圧力を示しています。セクター全体の平均スコアは64.1/100に達し、これは市場の方向性における強い優位性を示唆しています。このセクターに含まれる45銘柄のうち、40銘柄が現在強気シグナルを発しており、弱気シグナルを発している銘柄はゼロです。

この広範な強気傾向は、マクロ環境におけるWTI原油価格の堅調な動きに支えられています。原油価格は現在90.41ドル [データ: 2026-09-02 終値]で推移しており、エネルギー企業の収益性と市場センチメントに直接的な影響を与えています。セクター全体のスコアが60を上回っていることは、広範な買い意欲が存在することを示しています。

### なぜHaruspexデータはエネルギー株に強気を示しているのか？

Haruspexデータがエネルギー株に対して強気を示す主な理由は、特定の分析ディメンションにおける継続的な強気シグナルの蓄積にあります。特に、市場マイクロ構造と決算インテリジェンスがセクター全体の圧力を押し上げています。これは、伝統的な単一要素のスクリーニングでは見逃されがちな、市場行動の深層に存在する複数の要因を捉えています。

## 主要なエネルギー株の動向

Haruspexの分析は、以下の主要なエネルギー株に特に注目しています。

### **Marathon Petroleum Corporation (MPC)**

**MPC**は73.0という高いスコアを記録し、4.0ポイントの上昇を見せました。この強気圧力は、市場マイクロ構造のディメンションによって主に支えられています。データは**MPC**に対して63%の買いフローを示しており、これは明確な買い圧力を反映しています。また、タイトなスプレッドと優れた流動性も、この銘柄の健全な市場構造を示しています。ボリュームと注文フローを監視し、このシグナルが継続するかどうかを確認することが重要です。

### **HF Sinclair Corporation (DINO)**

**DINO**のスコアは72.0で、2.0ポイント上昇しました。この銘柄に対する強気圧力の主な要因は、決算インテリジェンスのディメンションです。HF Sinclair Corporationは最近のEPS予想を30.9%上回り、前年同期比で83.8%という驚異的なEPS成長を達成しました。この強い決算実績は市場にポジティブな影響を与えており、今後の決算関連シグナルに注目し、このトレンドが続くかを確認する必要があります。

### **Energy Transfer LP (ET)**

**ET**はスコア72.0、6.0ポイントの大幅な上昇を記録しました。この銘柄の強気圧力は、アルゴリズムセンチメント分析によって検出されています。Haruspexのシステムは、市場の広範なセンチメントデータからポジティブな方向性シグナルを抽出しています。このセンチメントの動きが、現在の強気圧力を支える主要因となっています。

### **Frontline plc (FRO)**

**FRO**は12.0ポイントも上昇し、スコア72.0となりました。この急上昇の背景には、市場マイクロ構造のディメンションにおける強い強気圧力が存在します。**FRO**は均衡の取れた注文フローを示しており、優れた流動性とタイトなスプレッドは、活発で健全な取引環境を示唆しています。ボリュームと注文フローの継続的なモニタリングが、この強力なシグナルを検証する鍵となります。

### **Chevron Corporation (CVX)**

**CVX**はスコア71.0、2.0ポイント上昇し、強気の見通しを維持しています。**CVX**に対する強気圧力もまた、市場マイクロ構造のディメンションに起因します。データは62%の買いフローを示しており、明確な買い圧力が存在することを示しています。優れた流動性とタイトなスプレッドも、このメジャープレーヤーの市場の健全性を裏付けています。ボリュームと注文フローは引き続き監視の対象です。

## レジーム閾値

現在のエネルギーセクターに対する強気の見通しは、以下のマクロ経済的条件に依存します。WTI原油価格が90ドルを超えて推移する限り、セクター全体の収益性には順風が吹くと考えられます。もしWTIが85ドルを下回って定着した場合、この強気圧力は弱まる可能性があります。

また、10年物米国債利回りが4.75%を上回る水準で維持されることは、インフレ期待と経済成長への信頼をある程度示し、エネルギー需要を支えるでしょう。VIXが16以下に留まる限り、市場のボラティリティは穏やかで、エネルギー株への投資家心理は安定しているとHaruspexデータは判断します。

## リスク要因

エネルギーセクターは、固有のリスク要因に晒されています。第一に、地政学的緊張がWTI原油価格に急速な変動をもたらす可能性があります。予期せぬ供給中断や需要の変化は、価格の大きな下方圧力につながる可能性があります。

第二に、グローバル経済の減速は、エネルギー需要の減少を引き起こす可能性があります。これは特に産業用途や輸送部門の燃料消費に影響し、セクター全体の収益見通しを悪化させるでしょう。第三に、規制環境の変化やクリーンエネルギーへの移行圧力の増加は、長期的なセクターの見通しに影響を与える可能性があります。

## この見解を覆すもの

現在の強気圧力に関する見解が変化する可能性のある具体的な条件を以下に示します。もしWTI原油価格が85ドル以下で終値を記録し、それが連続して5取引日続いた場合、セクターへの下方圧力が再評価されるでしょう。

また、セクター全体の平均Haruspexスコアが55を下回り、かつ強気シグナルの銘柄数が25%未満に減少した場合、市場センチメントの広範なシフトを示唆します。S&P 500が7500のサポートレベルを下回って終値を記録し、VIXが20を超える水準で定着した場合も、より広範な市場の不確実性がエネルギーセクターに波及し、現在の見通しを無効にする可能性があります。

## 今後の見通し

Haruspexデータは、短中期的にエネルギーセクターが市場において強い方向性的な優位性を維持すると予測しています。WTI原油価格の堅調な推移と、個々の銘柄における市場マイクロ構造および決算インテリジェンスからの継続的な強気シグナルは、セクター全体にポジティブなトレーディングエッジを提供しています。投資家は、トップ銘柄に見られる具体的なディメンションの動きに注目し、マクロ経済の閾値を監視することが推奨されます。

**TL;DR:** The Energy sector is currently experiencing strong bullish market pressure, indicated by an average Haruspex score of 64.1/100 and 40 bullish signals. Investors should monitor key names like MPC and DINO for continued directional momentum, supported by favorable macro conditions.

On Wednesday, September 2, 2026 ET, the Energy sector demonstrated significant bullish market pressure, with Haruspex analysis assigning an average directional score of 64.1 out of 100. This strong signal places the sector firmly in bullish territory, with 40 out of 45 analyzed stocks exhibiting positive directional pressure and no bearish signals detected.

## Top Energy Sector Signals

Several individual stocks within the Energy sector are contributing significantly to this overall bullish market pressure. These names showcase diverse drivers, from market microstructure to earnings performance, highlighting the sector's underlying strength.

**MPC** (Marathon Petroleum Corporation) scored 73.0, marking a 4.0-point increase. Its Market Microstructure dimension detected substantial bullish pressure, evidenced by a 63% buy flow and excellent liquidity with tight spreads. This suggests active accumulation by market participants.

**DINO** (HF Sinclair Corporation) registered a score of 72.0, up 2.0 points. The Earnings Intelligence dimension was a key driver for **DINO**, reporting an impressive EPS beat of 30.9% and an 83.8% year-over-year EPS growth. These figures indicate robust financial performance attracting investor attention.

**ET** (Energy Transfer LP) saw its score increase by 6.0 points to 72.0. Bullish pressure for **ET** stemmed from its Algorithmic Sentiment Analysis dimension, which captures positive automated market perceptions and trading signals. This often precedes broader market recognition.

**CVX** (Chevron Corporation) also showed strong directional signals, holding a score of 71.0 after a 2.0-point increase. Similar to **MPC**, **CVX**'s Market Microstructure dimension detected buying pressure, characterized by a 62% buy flow and excellent liquidity, indicating consistent demand.

Other notable names reflecting bullish market pressure include **FRO** (Frontline plc) at 72.0, driven by Market Microstructure, and **OXY** (Occidental Petroleum) at 71.0, also supported by Market Microstructure with balanced order flow. **SLB** (Schlumberger Limited) maintained a score of 71.0, influenced by bullish Short Interest & Lending Intelligence, showing a significant drop in shorted float.

### What Macro Factors Support Energy's Bullish Trend?

The prevailing macro environment provides a solid foundation for the Energy sector's bullish market pressure. WTI Crude Oil currently stands at $90.45 [data: 2026-09-02 close], a level that supports profitability for many energy producers and refiners. A relatively stable VIX at 15.38 [data: 2026-09-02 close] suggests contained market volatility.

The 10-Year Treasury Yield at 4.80% [data: 2026-09-02 close] also indicates a sustained rate environment, which can affect long-term project financing but does not appear to be an immediate headwind. The US Dollar Index (DXY) at 99.53 [data: 2026-09-02 close] shows a dollar that is firm but not overly strong to suppress commodity prices significantly.

## Sector Drivers

The primary drivers pushing the Energy sector's performance relative to the broader market are rooted in specific Haruspex dimensions. Market Microstructure signals are exceptionally strong across the sector, detecting persistent buying pressure and robust liquidity for numerous stocks. This reflects active institutional and retail interest.

Earnings Intelligence is another key factor, with several companies reporting stronger-than-expected results and significant year-over-year growth. This validates fundamental strength and attracts investment. Algorithmic Sentiment Analysis also plays a role, indicating positive automated trading signals that often foreshadow further upside.

## Regime Thresholds

The current bullish thesis for the Energy sector holds as long as WTI Crude Oil prices maintain above the $85 per barrel level. A sustained break below this threshold could introduce significant margin pressure for producers and refiners, weakening current signals.

Furthermore, if the 10-Year Treasury Yield were to experience a rapid and sustained increase above 5.00%, it could heighten capital costs for energy infrastructure projects and potentially impact investor sentiment across growth-sensitive sectors, including some segments of Energy.

## Risk Factors

Despite the current bullish market pressure, the Energy sector faces inherent risks. A significant global economic slowdown could depress demand for crude oil and refined products, leading to a decline in commodity prices and eroding profitability.

Geopolitical instability, while sometimes a short-term catalyst for higher oil prices, can also disrupt supply chains and create operational uncertainties. Lastly, unexpected shifts in environmental regulations or energy policy could impose new costs or restrict future exploration and production.

## What Would Change This View

The current bullish market pressure thesis for the Energy sector would be invalidated under specific measurable conditions. If WTI Crude Oil consistently closes below $85 for a full trading week, it would signal weakening market fundamentals and likely reverse positive market microstructure signals.

Another shift would occur if multiple leading Energy sector companies report significant earnings misses or downward revisions in their outlooks, negating the positive signals from Earnings Intelligence. A broad market correction, where the S&P 500 falls below its 50-day moving average on above-average volume, could also pull the sector lower.

## Outlook

The Haruspex data indicates a compelling bullish outlook for the Energy sector, driven by strong average scores and widespread positive directional signals. Favorable macro conditions, particularly sustained oil prices, support this view. While risks persist, the confluence of robust market microstructure and fundamental earnings strength suggests continued upward pressure for the near term.

Investors should continue to monitor the identified dimensions for any shifts, especially in buying pressure and earnings momentum. The sector's current posture offers a strong trading edge compared to the broader market, provided key commodity price thresholds are maintained.

### Stock Scores

**TL;DR:** Market dynamics on September 2, 2026, show significant pressure from WTI crude oil prices near $90 and 10-year Treasury yields above 4.7%. These macro forces are influencing a range of stocks, including **CRWD**, **DVN**, and **SNAP**, despite internal bullish signals for each company.

On 2026-09-02 ET, market dynamics were primarily shaped by persistent upward pressure in energy prices and rising interest rates. The S&P 500 closed down 0.71% at 7631.47 [data: 2026-09-02 close], while the NASDAQ declined 1.03% to 26099.77 [data: 2026-09-02 close]. The Russell 2000 also fell, indicating broad market weakness.

Haruspex analysis has identified two dominant themes driving market movements today. These themes, Energy & Commodities and Interest Rate Pressure, each carry a strength score of 65/100. This indicates significant directional influence across various sectors and individual equities.

## Energy & Commodities: Oil Near $90 Fuels Market Pressure

The Energy & Commodities theme is a primary driver today, with WTI Crude Oil trading at $89.85 [data: 2026-09-02 close]. This level, above the $85 threshold, signals continued cost headwinds for many industries and potential tailwinds for the energy sector. The theme’s strength registers at 65/100.

The broader energy sector reflects this bullish pressure. Of 38 energy stocks analyzed, 36 showed bullish pressure, with an average score of 65.5. This contrasts with other sectors where bullish signals are less pervasive.

Several stocks are significantly affected by this commodity dynamic. **CrowdStrike Holdings** (**CRWD**) registered a score of 53.0, following an -18.0 change. While the overall score change indicates bearish pressure, Institutional Ownership Intelligence currently adds bullish pressure for **CRWD**. The company reports 76.5% institutional ownership across 3,161 institutions, with net institutional buying detected.

Similarly, **Devon Energy Corporation** (**DVN**) saw its score at 55.0 after a -14.0 change. Earnings Intelligence provides bullish pressure for **DVN**, as the company beat EPS estimates by 10.6%. A strong_buy consensus from 26 analysts also supports this outlook, providing counter-pressure to the broader bearish macro theme.

**Snap Inc.** (**SNAP**) recorded a score of 53.0, also with a -14.0 change. Options Flow Intelligence currently adds bullish pressure for **SNAP**, with its options showing a bullish sentiment indicated by a Put/Call ratio of 0.22. This low put/call ratio suggests bullish positioning, contributing to a neutral outlook despite the negative score change.

## Interest Rate Pressure: 10Y Yield Above 4.7% Signals Caution

The second dominant theme is Interest Rate Pressure, with the 10-Year Treasury Yield at 4.78% [data: 2026-09-02 close]. This figure, exceeding the 4.5% threshold, indicates a higher cost of capital and potential valuation compression for growth-oriented assets. This theme also measures a strength of 65/100.

Rising yields often present challenges for sectors reliant on future earnings growth. Technology and communication services, for example, tend to be more sensitive to these shifts. The technology sector, with 87 stocks, has an average score of 56.8, indicating moderate directional pressure.

The same stocks impacted by energy prices are also seeing effects from interest rate pressure. **CrowdStrike Holdings** (**CRWD**), a technology firm, experiences bearish pressure from the rising rate environment. However, the bullish Institutional Ownership Intelligence, with significant net institutional buying, balances this out, leading to a neutral directional edge.

**Devon Energy Corporation** (**DVN**), while an energy stock, also faces broader market sentiment affected by rates. Its Earnings Intelligence, driven by an EPS beat and strong analyst consensus, acts as a specific counter-signal. This helps maintain a neutral outlook despite the bearish macro forces.

**Snap Inc.** (**SNAP**), in communication services, is particularly sensitive to interest rate fluctuations. The observed bearish score change is consistent with this. However, the bullish Options Flow Intelligence, reflecting a low put/call ratio, provides a significant counteracting force, leading to a neutral overall outlook.

### Why did CRWD, DVN, and SNAP show neutral outlooks despite bearish theme pressure?

The Haruspex system detected significant bearish score changes for **CRWD** (-18.0), **DVN** (-14.0), and **SNAP** (-14.0). These changes align with the strong macro headwinds from rising oil prices and interest rates. However, for each of these stocks, specific internal dimensions presented strong bullish counter-signals.

For **CRWD**, Institutional Ownership Intelligence indicated substantial net institutional buying. **DVN** benefited from strong Earnings Intelligence, including an EPS beat and a 'strong_buy' analyst consensus. **SNAP** displayed bullish Options Flow Intelligence, characterized by a low Put/Call ratio. These individual dimension strengths collectively offset the broad macro bearish pressure, resulting in a neutral directional edge for each stock.

## Cross-Theme Connections: Inflation and Capital Costs Converge

These two dominant themes are interconnected. Higher crude oil prices directly contribute to inflationary pressures across the economy. In response, central banks may sustain or tighten monetary policy, keeping interest rates elevated. This creates a feedback loop where energy costs drive inflation, which in turn necessitates higher rates.

The combined effect is a challenging environment for many companies. Businesses face increased operational costs from energy, alongside higher borrowing costs for expansion or managing debt. This dual pressure impacts profit margins and valuation multiples, particularly for growth stocks with earnings weighted heavily into the future.

## Regime Thresholds

The current market thesis, characterized by pressure from energy prices and interest rates, holds under specific conditions. For the Energy & Commodities theme, sustained WTI crude oil prices above $85 per barrel indicate continued bullish pressure for the energy sector and cost concerns for others. A drop below $80 would significantly weaken this theme.

Regarding Interest Rate Pressure, the 10-Year Treasury Yield remaining above 4.5% signals ongoing challenges for growth-sensitive sectors. A decisive break below 4.3% would alleviate some of this pressure, potentially shifting market dynamics towards higher valuation multiples for technology and similar stocks.

## What Would Change This View

Several measurable conditions could invalidate the current market thesis driven by these two themes. For the Energy & Commodities theme, if WTI crude oil closes below $80 for three consecutive trading days, the bullish directional pressure on energy stocks would likely dissipate. This would also ease inflation concerns.

Regarding Interest Rate Pressure, a significant and sustained drop in the 10-Year Treasury Yield could alter the outlook. Specifically, if the 10Y yield closes below 4.3% by the end of the week, it would signal a potential reversal in the macro rate environment. This would likely benefit growth sectors.

For individual stocks like **CRWD**, **DVN**, and **SNAP**, a clear shift in their respective internal bullish dimensions would change their neutral outlook. For instance, if institutional ownership data for **CRWD** showed net selling, or if **DVN** missed future EPS estimates, or if **SNAP's** options flow turned bearish, their directional edge could shift.

## Outlook: Navigating Dual Macro Headwinds

The market on September 2, 2026, is clearly under the influence of two powerful macro forces: elevated energy prices and rising interest rates. These themes currently contribute strong directional market pressure, impacting overall sector performance and individual equity movements.

While these macro headwinds exert bearish pressure on many stocks, Haruspex analysis shows that specific, named internal factors can provide significant counter-signals. This is evident in the neutral outlooks for **CRWD**, **DVN**, and **SNAP**, where bullish institutional ownership, earnings intelligence, and options flow respectively balance the external pressures.

Investors should continue to monitor WTI crude oil prices and 10-year Treasury yields closely. Observing how specific companies navigate these dual pressures, particularly those with strong internal dimension signals, will be key to understanding the market's evolving directional edge.

## Breaking Momentum: Key Stock Movements

Cybersecurity leader **CRWD** saw its Haruspex score fall by 18 points, moving from a previously higher bullish stance to a neutral 53.0. ### Devon Energy (DVN): Energy Sector Pressure Shifts

The oil and gas producer **DVN** experienced a 14-point drop in its Haruspex score, settling at a neutral 55.0. This decrease suggests a notable reduction in bullish market pressure, despite current Earnings Intelligence adding bullish pressure. The sudden score reduction points to potential counter-signals emerging from other market dynamics.

### ServiceNow (NOW) and Element Solutions (ESI): Short Covering Signals Muted

Both **NOW** and **ESI** experienced 13-point drops in their Haruspex scores, landing at 60.0 (bullish) and 54.0 (neutral), respectively. In contrast to the declines, shipping company **FRO** saw its score increase by 12 points, pushing it into strong bullish territory at 72.0. This robust upward movement signals growing bullish market pressure. Market Microstructure analysis for **FRO** currently adds bullish pressure, detecting balanced order flow and excellent liquidity with tight spreads. This suggests active buying interest and efficient trading conditions supporting the positive momentum.

## Why This Matters: Interpreting Rapid Score Shifts

Haruspex scores are designed to detect dynamic shifts in market directional pressure, not fundamental value. Large, sudden changes—like the 18-point drop for **CRWD** or the 12-point rise for **FRO**—often precede corresponding movements in stock price. These shifts signal a change in the collective behavior of market participants. Such rapid adjustments can indicate a re-evaluation of short-term prospects, a reaction to new information, or a change in technical positioning.

### Why are rapid score shifts significant for investors?

Rapid score shifts are significant because they highlight emerging divergences between a stock's underlying fundamentals and its immediate market sentiment. While a company's long-term outlook may remain stable, a sudden change in detected market pressure often precedes shifts in price action. This allows investors to react to evolving market dynamics more promptly than traditional fundamental analysis alone.

## Regime Thresholds: Monitoring the Macro Backdrop

The current market thesis operates within a defined macroeconomic landscape. As long as the S&P 500 remains above 7600 [data: 2026-09-02 close], overall market sentiment may retain a degree of stability. A sustained VIX below 20 [data: 2026-09-02 close] implies lower broad market volatility, potentially allowing individual stock dynamics to play out more clearly.

Conversely, a sustained breach of WTI Crude Oil above $90.00 [data: 2026-09-02 close] could introduce renewed inflationary pressures and impact energy-sensitive sectors. A move in the 10-Year Treasury Yield above 5.0% [data: 2026-09-02 close] could tighten financial conditions, affecting growth-oriented stocks. These levels serve as critical indicators for the broader market environment influencing specific stock movements.

## Contrarian View: Are These Moves Sustainable?

While Haruspex detects current market pressure, no signal is absolute or immune to reversal. The sudden drops for **CRWD**, **DVN**, and **SNAP** could represent temporary corrections rather than sustained bearish reversals. Similarly, **FRO**’s bullish surge might attract profit-taking or encounter unexpected headwinds. Market dynamics are inherently fluid, and counter-trends often emerge.

The bullish signals within specific dimensions—like institutional accumulation for **CRWD** or short covering for **NOW**—could reassert themselves and potentially reverse the overall score decline if other contributing factors shift. Constant vigilance for confirming signals or new opposing data is essential.

## What Would Change This View

Several specific conditions could invalidate the current directional signals. For **CRWD**, a recovery of its Haruspex score above 60.0 and sustained net institutional buying would suggest a renewed bullish bias. For **FRO**, a drop below its 60.0 bullish threshold, coupled with weakening market microstructure signals, would indicate a reversal of its upward momentum.

Broadly, if the S&P 500 experiences a sustained close below 7500 [data: 2026-09-02 close] on higher volume, the overall market pressure could shift bearish, potentially overriding individual stock signals. A spike in the VIX above 20 [data: 2026-09-02 close] would signal increased market uncertainty, potentially leading to greater volatility across all equities.

## Action Items: Monitoring the Next Moves

Investors should closely monitor the price action for **CRWD** in the coming days for signs of consolidation or further declines, especially noting any changes in institutional flow. For **FRO**, continued strong order flow and liquidity, as indicated by its Market Microstructure dimension, will be key to sustaining its bullish pressure.

For **DVN**, **SNAP**, **NOW**, and **ESI**, observe if the underlying bullish dimension signals (earnings, options flow, short interest) can reassert themselves to push their overall scores higher. Watching for follow-through in these specific dimensions can confirm or weaken the observed shifts in market pressure.

## リスク要因

- 世界の経済成長の鈍化は、原油需要に悪影響を与え、エネルギーセクターの銘柄に圧力をかける可能性があります。WTI原油価格の急落は、現在の強気な見通しを覆すでしょう。

- 地政学的緊張やサプライチェーンの新たな混乱は、市場のボラティリティを高め、投資家のリスク回避行動を促す可能性があります。これは流動性の高い銘柄にも影響を及ぼします。

## この見方を変えるもの

- S&P 500が7500の主要な支持水準を下回って週を終え、大幅な出来高を伴う場合、広範な市場の弱気な方向性圧力が強まります。
- VIXが持続的に20を超えて上昇し、市場の恐怖と不確実性が高まっていることを示す場合、現在の強気シグナルは覆される可能性があります。
- WTI原油が継続的に$85を下回って取引され、エネルギーセクターの収益見通しが大幅に悪化する場合、**MPC**や**DINO**などの主要な強気銘柄の市場圧力は弱まります。

2026年9月1日ET、Haruspexの分析は、エネルギーサービス大手Schlumberger (**SLB**) に注目すべき動きを捉えました。同社の総合スコアは72.0から76.0へと4.0ポイント上昇し、現在、強い強気方向性圧力を示唆しています。

## 現在のマクロ環境

| 指標 | 現在値 | 前日比 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| S&P 500 | 7628.59 | -0.75% |
| VIX | 15.98 | - |
| 米国10年債利回り | 4.78% | - |
| WTI原油 | $88.19 | - |

このスコア上昇の主な要因は、**SLB**の市場マイクロストラクチャー次元の強力なパフォーマンスです。この次元は82/100を記録し、市場行動における強い強気圧力を継続して検出しています。これは、従来の分析では見過ごされがちな、買い手の緊急性と市場参加者の行動を反映しています。

Haruspexデータは、**SLB**が平均を325%上回る出来高で85%の買いフローを示していることを明らかにしました。これは、市場の動きを形成する上で重要な役割を果たす、大規模な買い需要を示唆しています。株価は$59.67で取引を終え、この日は0.75%下落しましたが、過去52週間のレンジは$31.64から$60.46です [データ: 2026-09-01 close]。

### SLBのマイクロストラクチャー・スコア82は何を意味するのか？

**SLB**のマイクロストラクチャー・スコアが82であることは、市場が株価を上方へ押し上げる明確な圧力を受けていることを示します。これは単なる買い推奨ではなく、市場参加者が積極的にポジションを構築しているという、具体的な行動の観察です。これは、日中の価格変動の裏にあるフローを分析することで明らかになります。

従来の単一要因スクリーンでは、出来高の急増や日中の価格変動を捉えることはできても、その背後にある買い圧力の強さや継続性までを特定することは困難です。Haruspexのマイクロストラクチャー次元は、これらの詳細な市場行動を数値化し、買い手の優位性を示します。

## 次元別の洞察

マイクロストラクチャー次元が82という傑出したスコアである一方、他の次元も**SLB**の市場圧力を形成しています。

- **テクニカル (68/100):** この次元は強気圧力を示し、現在の価格トレンドが継続する可能性を強調しています。
- **ショートインタレスト (65/100):** 空売り残高に関連する指標は、中立的な範囲の上限にあり、潜在的なショートカバーの可能性を示唆しています。
- **競合他社 (64/100):** 競合他社に対する相対的なパフォーマンスも、中程度の強気圧力を反映しています。
- **センチメント (64/100):** 広範な市場センチメントは、**SLB**に対してやや強気な傾向を示しています。
- **オプションフロー (63/100):** オプション市場の活動も強気側の偏りを示しており、機関投資家のポジショニングを示唆しています。

一方、決算 (58/100) および機関投資家 (57/100) の次元は、中立的な範囲の中央に位置しています。これらの次元は、現時点では**SLB**に対して明確な方向性優位性を示しておらず、市場の強い動きの主要な推進力ではありません。しかし、安定したスコアは、これらの領域に大きな逆風がないことを示しています。

## レジーム閾値

**SLB**に対する現在の強気な見通しは、いくつかのマクロ環境要因によって影響を受けます。WTI原油価格が$85以上で推移し続ける限り、エネルギーサービス部門には追い風が吹くでしょう。現在の原油価格は$88.19 [データ: 2026-09-01 close] であり、これは**SLB**の収益性にとって好ましい環境です。

VIX指数が18を下回って推移し、市場全体のボラティリティが抑制されることも、強気圧力の継続をサポートします。また、米国10年債利回りが5.0%を超えて持続的に上昇しない限り、資本コストの上昇が**SLB**のような設備投資が必要な企業に与える影響は限定的でしょう。

## リスク要因

**SLB**の現在の市場圧力は強いものの、リスクは存在します。もしWTI原油価格が急落し、エネルギーセクターへの投資意欲が減退した場合、現在の買い圧力は弱まる可能性があります。また、決算と機関投資家の次元は現在中立であり、これらの領域で予期せぬ悪材料が発生した場合、スコアの低下を招く恐れがあります。

全体的な市場センチメントやオプションフローのスコアが現在60台中盤であるため、これらが急速に悪化すれば、総合スコアに下向きの圧力がかかる可能性もあります。監視を継続し、これらの次元の変化に注意を払うことが重要です。

## この見方を変更する条件

Haruspexの**SLB**に対する強気な方向性圧力のテーゼは、以下の具体的な条件で無効化される可能性があります。

- **SLB**のマイクロストラクチャー次元スコアが45を下回り、かつ買いフローが持続的に50%を下回った場合。
- WTI原油価格が連続する5取引日で$80を下回って終値となり、それが短期的なトレンドの反転を示唆する場合。
- **SLB**が平均出来高の150%以上で、現在の価格から5%以上の下落を伴う売りフローが連続して発生した場合。

## 今後の見通し

Haruspexのデータは、**SLB**が引き続き強い買い圧力の恩恵を受ける可能性が高いことを示しています。特にマイクロストラクチャー次元の持続的な強さは、市場参加者がこの株に対して積極的にポジションを構築していることを強調します。原油価格が現在の水準を維持し、マクロ経済の逆風が限定的であれば、この強気な市場圧力は継続するでしょう。

投資家は、買い圧力の持続性と、出来高のフォローアップに注目すべきです。これにより、この市場行動の強度が確認されるか、あるいは弱まるかが示されます。データ駆動型の洞察は、市場のセンチメントとフローが現在のところ**SLB**にとって有利であることを示唆しています。

## Schlumberger (SLB) Sees Strong Bullish Pressure at 76.0

This strong bullish signal is primarily driven by robust Microstructure activity, showing dominant buying pressure on elevated volume. Investors should monitor for continued buy flow to confirm the trend.

On Tuesday, September 1, 2026 ET, Schlumberger (**SLB**) registered a strong bullish market pressure score of 76.0, an increase of 4.0 points from its previous score of 72.0. This move occurred as the broader market saw declines, with the S&P 500 at 7629.09, down -0.74% [data: 2026-09-01 close].

| Metric | Current | Key Level |
| --- | --- | --- |

| US Dollar Index (DXY) | 99.61 | 100 (strength indicator) |

The core of **SLB**'s elevated market pressure stems from its Microstructure dimension, which scored a robust 82/100. This dimension detected substantial buying pressure, with 85% buy flow occurring on volume 325% above average. This pattern suggests concentrated demand for **SLB** shares.

### What does SLB's 82/100 Microstructure score signify?

A Microstructure score of 82/100 for **SLB** indicates a high degree of order book imbalance favoring buyers, alongside unusually strong trading activity. This specific signal points to active accumulation rather than passive trading. Such strong directional flow often precedes significant price moves, reflecting institutional or large-scale retail interest.

Beyond Microstructure, several other Haruspex dimensions also supported a bullish outlook for **SLB**. The Technical dimension scored 68/100, indicating favorable price action and trend strength. Short-Interest measured at 65/100, suggesting limited bearish speculative positioning. Sentiment also registered 64/100, reflecting generally positive market discourse.

The Options-Flow dimension, at 63/100, contributed to the bullish pressure by detecting activity in the options market that aligns with an upward price trajectory for **SLB**. The Competitors dimension, scoring 64/100, suggested that **SLB**'s relative position within its sector was also favorable, adding to its directional edge.

Conventional single-factor screens often focus on fundamental metrics or basic technical indicators, potentially missing the subtle yet powerful signals of active market participation. Haruspex's integration of Microstructure, Options-Flow, and Short-Interest dimensions, alongside Technical and Sentiment, provides a more comprehensive view of immediate market dynamics around **SLB**. This allows the identification of concentrated buying pressure that might not be evident from simple price charts or news headlines alone.

## Risk Factors and Nuances

While **SLB** exhibits strong bullish pressure, some dimensions show a more neutral stance, presenting potential risks. The Earnings dimension scored 58/100, indicating that recent earnings-related data does not currently offer a strong directional edge, either positive or negative. Similarly, the Institutional dimension, at 57/100, suggests institutional activity is not a dominant driver of directional pressure at this moment.

The current macro environment also presents potential headwinds. The S&P 500 declined -0.74% [data: 2026-09-01 close], signaling broader market weakness. While WTI Crude Oil at $88.24 supports the energy sector, any significant downturn could impact sentiment and revenue for oilfield services companies like **SLB**.

## Regime Thresholds

The bullish thesis for **SLB** remains robust as long as WTI Crude Oil stays above $80. This level generally supports sustained exploration and production activity, which directly benefits oilfield services providers. A 10Y Treasury Yield below 5.00% also provides a more favorable borrowing environment for capital-intensive sectors, including energy.

## What Would Change This View

- A sustained reversal in **SLB**'s Microstructure signals, specifically if buy flow drops significantly below 50% on above-average volume, indicating a shift from accumulation to distribution.
- WTI Crude Oil closing below $80 for a full week, signaling a potentially bearish shift in the broader energy market that would pressure **SLB**'s operational outlook.
- A confirmed breach of key technical support levels for **SLB**, coupled with negative sentiment shifts, which would invalidate the current Technical and Sentiment directional signals.

## Outlook for SLB

Haruspex data indicates a clear and strong bullish market pressure for **SLB** heading into the coming weeks. The dominant Microstructure score, supported by positive signals from Technical, Short-Interest, and Options-Flow dimensions, points to continued upward momentum. The current price of **SLB** at $59.59 [data: 2026-09-01 close] sits near its 52-week high of $60.46, with the observed buying pressure suggesting potential for further appreciation.

Monitoring the continued strength in buy flow and sustained volume will be crucial in confirming the longevity of this directional signal. While general market weakness and neutral signals in Earnings and Institutional dimensions bear watching, the current data leans heavily towards a bullish trajectory for **SLB**.

### What do these sharp score changes indicate?

Haruspex scores measure detected market directional pressure, not company quality. A score change of 10 points or more, especially in a single trading session, indicates a rapid shift in how the market is behaving around a particular stock. These shifts can stem from various factors, including evolving sentiment, changes in institutional activity, or microstructure dynamics, often preceding sustained price moves.

## Why This Matters

Large score swings historically point to a re-evaluation of a stock's immediate trading trajectory by market participants. Such rapid changes provide early signals that conventional, single-factor screens might miss. By identifying these shifts, Haruspex provides insight into where market pressure is building or receding, offering a view of potential short-term to medium-term directional bias.

## Regime Thresholds

The current bullish signals for stocks like **CRWD** and **WDS** are supported by a moderately stable macro environment, indicated by the S&P 500 above 7,600 and VIX below 18.0. A sustained break below S&P 500 7,600 or a VIX spike above 18.0 could introduce broader market headwinds, potentially neutralizing these stock-specific bullish pressures. Rising 10Y Treasury yields above 5.0% or WTI Crude above $90.00 could also shift sector dynamics, impacting sentiment.

## Contrarian View: Could These Moves Reverse?

Rapid momentum shifts are susceptible to reversals. Profit-taking after significant upticks, unexpected news events, or broader market corrections could quickly unwind positive pressures. For stocks with declining scores, a swift change in underlying sentiment or institutional positioning could restore bullish momentum, especially if short covering intensifies beyond current levels.

## What Would Change This View

- If **CRWD** closes below its 50-day simple moving average for two consecutive trading days on volume exceeding its 20-day average, the current strong bullish pressure thesis would be deferred.
- Should **TXRH**'s short interest begin to rise again significantly, or if its trading volume drops sharply while the stock tests key support levels, the neutral outlook could shift back towards bearish pressure.
- A significant spike in the VIX above 20.0, indicating increased market volatility, could broadly dampen bullish sentiment across all sectors, invalidating individual stock momentum signals.

## Action Items

Investors should monitor several factors moving forward. For **CRWD**, observe volume trends and order flow for continued buying pressure. For **TXRH**, watch for further developments in short interest data and any changes in trading range. For **WDS** and **ESI**, sustained strong order flow and above-average volume would confirm current bullish signals. Macro indicators like the 10Y Treasury yield and WTI Crude Oil prices also warrant close attention, as their movements can impact broader market sentiment.

## マクロレジームモニター

主要なマクロ経済指標は、市場の複合的な状況を映し出しています。S&P 500はわずかに下落しましたが、VIXは比較的穏やかな水準を維持しており、市場全体のリスク選好は高止まりしています。

### レジーム閾値

現在の強気な個別株の圧力は、特定の市場環境下で最も有効です。S&P 500が7500ポイントを上回り、VIXが20未満で推移する限り、リスクオンの取引環境が続くと考えられます。WTI原油価格が90ドルを下回っていれば、インフレ圧力は管理可能な範囲に留まるでしょう。ただし、10年物米国債利回りが5.00%を超えて上昇すると、成長株に売り圧力がかかる可能性があります。

## Haruspexが検出したもの

今日の市場の動きを牽引したのは、主に「マイクロストラクチャー」および「センチメント」の各次元からの強いシグナルでした。これらの次元は、従来の単一要因スクリーンが見落としがちな、市場における買い手と売り手の具体的な圧力と投資家の心理を捉えます。

特に、マイクロストラクチャー次元は、高水準の買い注文フローと出来高の増加を識別しました。これは、特定の銘柄に対する即時の需要と流動性の高まりを示唆します。センチメント次元は、ソーシャルメディアやニュース分析を通じて、投資家のポジティブな感情の集中を検出し、その銘柄に対する関心の高まりを裏付けました。

## トップ銘柄が示す市場の方向性

Haruspexのデータは、複数の主要銘柄で強い買い圧力を検出しました。これらの銘柄は、その固有の特性と市場の次元からの肯定的なシグナルが組み合わさることで、市場全体のセンチメントの強さを体現しています。

### SLBの強い買い圧力とその要因とは？

**SLB**（Schlumberger Limited）は、76.0のHaruspexスコアを記録し、+4.0ポイントの増加を見せました。この日の終値は60.10ドルで、4.83%上昇しました [data: 2026-09-01 close]。この強い動きの主な要因は、マイクロストラクチャー次元が82という高いスコアを示したことです。これは、平均を325%上回る出来高で85%の買いフローが観測されたことを意味します。

また、テクニカル次元が68、ショートインタレスト次元が65と、それぞれ強気な圧力を追加しました。**SLB**の52週レンジは31.64ドルから60.46ドルであり、現在の価格はレンジの上限に近づいています。この動きの持続性を確認するためには、フォローアップの出来高と買い圧力の継続性を監視することが重要です。

### その他の注目銘柄

**DAR**（Darling Ingredients Inc.）は、Haruspexスコアが73.0と+10.0ポイント急上昇しました。終値は66.27ドルで、3.03%の上昇です [data: 2026-09-01 close]。マイクロストラクチャー次元が79、機関投資家次元が68、競合他社次元が67と、複数の次元で強い買い圧力を示しています。特に平均を164%上回る出来高で80%の買いフローが見られました。

**DE**（Deere & Company）も73.0のスコアで+8.0ポイント上昇し、終値は654.91ドルで3.90%高となりました [data: 2026-09-01 close]。マイクロストラクチャー次元が75、機関投資家次元が71、オプションフロー次元が63を記録し、69%の買いフローと優れた流動性が特徴です。

**ABBV**（AbbVie Inc.）は、Haruspexスコアが73.0、+2.0ポイントの上昇でした。終値は256.42ドルで、0.37%高です [data: 2026-09-01 close]。センチメント次元が69、マイクロストラクチャー次元が67、ショートインタレスト次元が65と、特に感情と注文フローの両面から強気な圧力が検出されています。

**NOW**（ServiceNow Inc.）は、73.0のスコアで+2.0ポイント上昇し、終値は147.99ドルで2.27%高となりました [data: 2026-09-01 close]。センチメント次元が75、マイクロストラクチャー次元が72、ショートインタレスト次元が66と、こちらも感情面と注文フローが強く影響しています。

**UBS**（UBS Group AG）と**XOM**（Exxon Mobil Corporation）もそれぞれ73.0のスコアを記録し、強い買い圧力が観測されました。これらの銘柄も、主にマイクロストラクチャーやセンチメントの次元が牽引要因となっています。

## リスク要因

- 市場センチメントは急速に変化する可能性があり、現在の買い圧力が持続しないリスクがあります。特にVIXが上昇し始めた場合、広範な市場の不確実性が高まります。
- 原油価格の変動は、エネルギー関連銘柄（**SLB**, **XOM**, **EC**, **SUN**）に直接的な影響を与えます。WTIが90ドルを超えて上昇すれば、コスト圧力が増大する可能性があります。
- 金利の上昇は、特に成長志向の企業（**NOW**）や負債の多い企業にとって、将来の収益性を圧迫する可能性があります。10年物米国債利回りの継続的な上昇は、リスク資産の魅力を低下させる可能性があります。

## この見解を覆すもの

現在の強気な個別株の方向性圧力に関する見解が変化する、または無効になる可能性のある具体的な条件は以下の通りです。

- S&P 500が7500ポイントを下回って週を終え、それが2週間連続で続いた場合、広範な市場の弱気センチメントが再燃するでしょう。
- VIXが20を明確に上回り、かつ数日間にわたってその水準を維持した場合、市場の不確実性と売り圧力が増大したと判断されます。
- 主要な個別銘柄（例: **SLB**, **DAR**, **DE**）のマイクロストラクチャー次元における買いフローの割合が50%を下回り、出来高が平均を下回る水準で継続した場合、現在の買い圧力が弱まったと見なされます。

## 市場の展望

Haruspexのデータは、S&P 500のわずかな変動にもかかわらず、多くの個別銘柄で強い買い圧力が持続していることを明確に示しています。マイクロストラクチャーやセンチメントの次元が引き続き強気なシグナルを発しており、短期的には個別銘柄に焦点を当てた取引機会が豊富である可能性が高いです。

しかし、マクロ経済指標、特に長期金利や原油価格の動きは注意深く監視する必要があります。これらの要因は、個別銘柄のパフォーマンスに大きな影響を与える可能性があります。投資家は、個々の銘柄のデータドリブンなシグナルと、広範な市場のリスク要因とのバランスを考慮することが求められます。

## Risk Factors

The current bullish signals, particularly those driven by microstructure, carry inherent risks. A sudden reversal in buying pressure, especially on significantly reduced volume, could rapidly diminish these signals. Broader market volatility, as measured by the VIX, remains a factor to watch, even at current moderate levels.

Elevated 10-Year Treasury Yields could pose a risk to growth stocks if they continue to climb, potentially shifting investor preference towards value-oriented sectors. Geopolitical developments impacting crude oil prices could also introduce volatility, particularly for energy-sector names like **SLB** and **XOM**. Unexpected earnings revisions or changes in analyst sentiment could also alter directional pressure.

## What Would Change This View

The current bullish thesis for the overall market would be challenged if the S&P 500 registers a weekly close below the 7500 level on above-average volume. This would suggest a weakening of broad market support that underpins individual stock strength.

For microstructure-driven signals, a sustained period of declining buy flow percentages for several consecutive days, coupled with sub-average trading volume, would indicate a dissipation of accumulation pressure. Specifically, if **SLB**'s buy flow drops below 60% for three consecutive days on declining volume, its strong bullish pressure thesis would be re-evaluated.

A move by the 10-Year Treasury Yield above 5.0% for a full week, combined with a VIX reading consistently above 20, could signal a broader market re-pricing. Such conditions would likely invalidate the prevailing bullish pressure observed in many current top stocks, especially those sensitive to interest rate expectations.

## Market Outlook

Haruspex analysis indicates that bullish directional pressure remains robust across the market, particularly within energy, industrials, and select technology and healthcare names. The emphasis on microstructure and sentiment dimensions suggests that active market participation and underlying positive perceptions are driving these movements. Investors should prioritize monitoring follow-through volume and order flow to confirm current directional signals.

While the broader market experiences minor pullbacks, the concentrated bullish signals suggest resilience in specific sectors. The moderate VIX and steady WTI Crude Oil prices provide a stable, albeit watchful, backdrop. Maintaining awareness of macro shifts, especially in interest rates, will be key to navigating the weeks ahead.

AIエクスポージャー（Ai-Exposure次元）の平均スコアは52.4で、**AMZN**、**NVDA**、**AAPL**などの銘柄におけるAI技術への投資と市場の期待が継続していることを示します。この次元は、現代市場においてその重要性を保っています。

## レジーム閾値

現在の市場の強気な見方は、いくつかの主要なマクロ指標によって裏付けられています。VIXが20を下回り、市場のボラティリティが抑制されている間、この強気傾向は維持されるでしょう。また、WTI原油が$90を下回っている限り、インフレ圧力は緩和され、企業の利益マージンへの逆風は抑えられます。

10年債利回りが5.00%を超えずに推移していることは、資金調達コストが管理可能であることを示しています。S&P 500が7700ポイントレベルを明確に突破することは、さらなる市場の上昇の触媒となる可能性があります。

## リスク要因

Haruspexの分析は、いくつかのリスク要因も浮き彫りにしています。インサイダー取引（Insider-Trading次元）の平均スコアは34.7と低く、**TSM**、**TSLA**、**AMZN**などの主要銘柄で内部関係者の買い圧力が不足している可能性を示唆します。これは、広範な市場の強気シグナルとは対照的です。

集中リスク（Concentration-Risk次元）は45.2と中立を下回っており、特定の大型銘柄への集中投資が市場全体の脆弱性を高める可能性を示しています。**UNH**、**TSM**、**BRK.B**がこの次元で注目されています。

暗号通貨（Crypto次元）の平均スコアは46.7で、このセクターが現在の市場の強気トレンドから乖離していることを示しています。**COIN**、**PYPL**、**WULF**といった銘柄は、より慎重なアプローチを要するかもしれません。

## 何がこの見方を変えるか

- もしS&P 500が週足で7500ポイントを下回り、取引量が過去30日平均を上回って引けた場合、現在の広範な強気圧力のテーゼは一時的に中断されるでしょう。
- VIXが20を明確に超えて終値をつける日が連続した場合、市場のボラティリティが上昇し、リスクオフセンチメントが高まることを示唆します。
- 10年債利回りが5.00%を継続的に上回る場合、将来の資金調達コストに対する懸念が高まり、成長株に逆風となる可能性があります。

## 市場の見通し

Haruspexのデータは、短期的には市場に継続的な強気圧力がかかることを示唆しています。空売りおよび規制関連の次元における高いスコアは、特定のセクターや銘柄に追い風が吹いていることを意味します。

機関投資家の安定した関心とポジティブなセンチメントは、市場の底堅さを支える要因です。ただし、インサイダー取引の弱さと集中リスクの存在は、市場参加者が慎重さを保つべき領域を指摘しています。

投資家は、これらの次元の動向を注意深く監視し、特に規制の変化や空売り比率の高い銘柄における動きに注目すべきです。マクロ経済指標の安定性は、現在の市場環境を維持するための重要な要素であり続けます。

*Source: [2026年8月31日：市場の強気圧力と空売り・規制トレンド](https://haruspex.guru/insights/market-trends-2026-08-31-ja)*

## August 31, 2026: Regulatory Signals Strengthen Amid Broad Bullish Pressure

**Category:** Market Trends | **Published:** 2026-08-31 | **Language:** en

> Haruspex analysis for August 31, 2026, reveals strengthening regulatory tailwinds and persistent bullish market pressure in key sectors.

### Stock Scores

| Symbol | Company | Score | Change | Outlook |
|--------|---------|-------|--------|---------|

| ABBV | AbbVie Inc. | 71.0 | +6.0 | bullish |
| NMR | Nomura Holdings, Inc. | 71.0 | +9.0 | bullish |

**TL;DR:** Haruspex analysis on 715 stocks shows a market leaning bullish, with 56% of analyzed tickers exhibiting upward directional pressure. The average market score stands at 57.0/100, driven significantly by strengthening regulatory signals and sustained short-interest dynamics.

On Monday, 2026-08-31 ET, the broader market showed a mixed picture as Haruspex analysis pointed to specific underlying strengths despite a slight dip in the S&P 500. The S&P 500 closed at 7670.98, down 0.53% [data: 2026-08-30 close], suggesting a day of consolidation.

Haruspex data indicates that regulatory dynamics are becoming a more prominent factor influencing market pressure. The average score for the regulatory dimension rose to 61.7, marking the strongest positive shift among all dimensions with a +1.7 change.

This signals increasing market anticipation of favorable policy environments or decreasing regulatory hurdles for specific companies and sectors. Top stocks exhibiting these regulatory signals include **V**, **MA**, and **AAPL**.

## Macro Regime Monitor

| Metric | Current | Inline Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| S&P 500 | 7670.98 (-0.53%) | [data: 2026-08-30 close] |
| VIX | 15.16 | [data: 2026-08-30 close] |
| 10Y Treasury Yield | 4.76% | [data: 2026-08-30 close] |
| WTI Crude Oil | $85.54 | [data: 2026-08-30 close] |
| US Dollar Index (DXY) | 99.41 | [data: 2026-08-30 close] |

The VIX, a measure of market volatility, held steady at 15.16 [data: 2026-08-30 close]. This level suggests an absence of immediate panic but keeps the door open for potential price swings. The 10Y Treasury Yield remained elevated at 4.76% [data: 2026-08-30 close], continuing to pose a consideration for growth-oriented sectors.

### What the Data Shows: Undercurrents of Bullish Pressure

Beyond regulatory trends, the market exhibits sustained bullish pressure from short-interest dynamics. The short-interest dimension averaged 62.1, the highest among all dimensions, indicating potential for short-covering rallies in specific names. Stocks like **AEG**, **SCHW**, and **NMR** show pronounced signals in this area.

The institutional dimension also registered a strong average score of 61.2, signaling continued professional accumulation or positioning in stocks such as **CR**, **SHOP**, and **MXL**. This suggests that large investors are maintaining or increasing their exposure, aligning with the broader bullish tilt.

Investor sentiment, as measured by the sentiment dimension, also supported this directional lean with an average score of 60.7. This indicates a generally positive disposition towards stocks like **AVGO**, **ET**, and **SNAP**, contributing to upward pressure.

While most dimensions remained stable, slight positive shifts were noted in management (average score 51.3, +0.3 change) and climate-risk (average score 52.7, +0.1 change). These subtle movements suggest incremental positive developments in corporate governance and environmental preparedness that the market is beginning to price in.

#### Regime Thresholds

The current bullish thesis largely holds as long as the VIX remains below 18.0, indicating a controlled volatility environment. If the 10Y Treasury Yield breaks sustainably below 4.50%, a renewed appetite for growth stocks might materialize, potentially amplifying current bullish signals.

Conversely, a sustained move in WTI Crude Oil above $90.00 would introduce significant inflationary pressure, potentially challenging corporate margins and dampening overall market sentiment, particularly for sectors with high energy dependencies.

## Risk Factors

Despite the prevailing bullish signals in several key dimensions, certain areas warrant caution. The S&P 500's modest decline on the trading day indicates that broad market enthusiasm is not universal, and selective pressures are present.

The insider-trading dimension recorded a notably low average score of 34.7, suggesting a lack of strong positive conviction from company insiders across the analyzed universe. This can act as a contrarian signal, indicating that those with the most intimate knowledge are not aggressively buying.

Furthermore, the concentration-risk dimension held a bearish average score of 45.2 for top stocks like **UNH**, **TSM**, and **BRK.B**. While not a strong directional indicator, it highlights potential vulnerabilities related to single-company or sector-specific dependencies within portfolios.

### What Would Change This View?

The current outlook for strengthening bullish pressure would be reconsidered under several specific conditions. If the S&P 500 were to close two consecutive weeks below its 50-day moving average on above-average volume, it would signal a potential reversal of the current consolidation trend.

A significant downturn in the short-interest dimension, falling below 55, would suggest that the potential for short-covering has largely abated or that new bearish catalysts are emerging. Similarly, a reversal in the regulatory dimension, with its average score dropping below 50, could indicate new headwinds from policy or government action.

## Outlook: Continued Vigilance with a Bullish Lean

The Haruspex analysis suggests a market where specific underlying strengths, particularly in regulatory and short-interest dynamics, are building positive market pressure. While the S&P 500 experienced a slight retreat, the overall sentiment and institutional positioning remain robust, driving a net bullish posture.

Investors should monitor the interplay between regulatory developments and short-interest activity, as these dimensions are currently providing the strongest directional signals. Vigilance regarding broader macro factors and the subdued insider trading activity remains prudent as these dynamics unfold in the near term.

## エネルギーセクターが市場の逆風に逆らい強気姿勢を維持：SLBがリード

2026年8月31日 ETの取引セッションでは、エネルギーセクターは顕著な強気圧力を示しました。S&P 500が7678.99ポイントで-0.42%の調整を見せた一方で [data: 2026-08-31 close]、Haruspexのデータはセクター全体にわたるポジティブなモメンタムを指摘しています。

## エネルギーセクター：市場の逆風を乗り越える

エネルギーセクターのHaruspex平均スコアは62.0と、強気の市場圧力を示しています。これは、セクター内の45銘柄中42銘柄で強気シグナルが検出され、弱気シグナルがゼロであったことによって裏付けられています。VIXが15.25 [data: 2026-08-31 close]と比較的落ち着いている一方で、S&P 500が小幅に下落している市場環境において、このセクターの明確な方向性は際立っています。

WTI原油価格は$85.38 [data: 2026-08-31 close]で推移しており、エネルギー企業の収益性を支える主要な要因となっています。10年物米国債利回りは4.76% [data: 2026-08-31 close]と高水準ですが、現在の原油価格水準はセクターの投資魅力に影響を与えていないようです。

### 主要な推進銘柄：データが示す強気な動き

セクター全体の強気な見通しに貢献しているのは、個別の銘柄からの強力なデータシグナルです。複数のHaruspex次元が、これらの企業の市場行動を駆動していることが示されています。

#### **SLB** (Schlumberger Limited)

**SLB**は72.0のスコアを記録し、2.0ポイント上昇しました。この銘柄の強力な強気圧力は、Market Microstructure次元によって検出されています。データは、平均を180%上回る取引量で85%の買いフローという買い圧力を示しています。この買い圧力の継続とそれに続く取引量が、このシグナルを強化するか弱めるかを判断する上で重要になります。

#### **FRO** (Frontline plc)

**FRO**は69.0のスコアを維持し、強力な強気シグナルを保持しています。Competitive Intelligence次元は、**FRO**の強気圧力を明確に示しています。同社は石油および製品タンカーの所有・運営に携わる海運会社であり、Oil & Gas Midstream業界で競合5社中、時価総額で1位にランクされています。

#### **COP** (ConocoPhillips)

**COP**は68.0のスコアで、6.0ポイントの著しい上昇を見せました。この上昇の主な要因はEarnings Intelligence次元にあります。ConocoPhillipsはEPS予想を14.1%上回り、前年同期比でEPSが70.5%増加しました。今後の収益情報シグナルの追跡は、この強気圧力を確認するために不可欠です。

#### **E** (Eni S.p.A.)

**E**は67.0のスコアで3.0ポイント上昇しました。Short Interest & Lending Intelligence次元は、この銘柄の強気圧力を示しています。Eni S.p.A.の浮動株に対する空売り比率は0.1%であり、前月から99.9%減少しています。これは空売りポジションの解消、すなわちショートカバーが進行中である可能性を示唆しており、潜在的な強気シグナルとして監視されます。

### 原油価格高騰はエネルギーセクターにどのような影響を与えるか？

WTI原油価格が$85.38 [data: 2026-08-31 close]で推移している現状は、エネルギーセクターにとって直接的な収益性の追い風となっています。原油価格の上昇は、探査・生産企業の利益率を改善させ、投資と生産拡大のインセンティブを高めます。また、海運（**FRO**など）やサービス（**SLB**など）といった関連産業にもプラスの影響を及ぼし、セクター全体の強気なセンチメントを後押しします。

## セクターの推進要因

Haruspexのデータは、Energyセクターが市場の他の部分と比較して特定の次元で強いパフォーマンスを示していることを明らかにしています。Earnings Intelligence次元は、複数の主要企業が予想を上回る業績を報告し、セクターのファンダメンタルズを強化していることを示唆しています。また、Market Microstructure次元も買いフローの増加と取引量の拡大を検出し、短期的な強気圧力を反映しています。

## レジーム閾値

- WTI原油価格が$80を下回る水準で週間終値を記録する場合、エネルギー企業の収益性に対する逆風が生じ、現在の強気テーシスが弱まる可能性があります。
- 10年物米国債利回りが5.0%を超える水準で持続的に推移する場合、借入コストの増加を通じてセクターの設備投資計画に影響を与え、全体的な成長見通しを抑制する可能性があります。

## リスク要因

エネルギーセクターの持続的な強気モメンタムは、いくつかのリスクに直面しています。第一に、地政学的緊張やOPEC+の生産決定による原油価格の急激な変動は、セクター全体の収益性と株価に直接的な影響を与えます。高ボラティリティは投資家心理を急速に冷やす可能性があります。

第二に、再生可能エネルギーへの移行圧力と政府の規制強化は、長期的に化石燃料への依存度を低下させる可能性があります。これは、特に探査・生産企業にとって、将来の成長見通しに対する構造的な逆風となるでしょう。

最後に、世界経済の成長鈍化はエネルギー需要の減少につながり、原油およびガス価格に下押し圧力をかける可能性があります。特に、主要消費国における経済活動の停滞は、セクターの収益見通しを悪化させる恐れがあります。

## この見方を変える要因

現在のエネルギーセクターに対する強気の見通しは、以下の具体的な条件下で無効となるでしょう。

- WTI原油価格が2週間連続で$80/バレルを下回る水準で取引を終えた場合、収益圧力が高まる可能性があります。
- S&P 500が過去30日間の平均出来高を25%上回る出来高で、週足終値が直近の安値を割り込んだ場合、市場全体のリスクオフ心理がエネルギーセクターにも波及する可能性があります。
- 主要なエネルギー企業（例：**SLB**、**COP**）が、次の四半期決算でEPS予想を平均10%以上下回る結果を出した場合、Earnings Intelligence次元からの強気シグナルは大きく弱まるでしょう。

## 今後の見通し

Haruspexのデータは、S&P 500が小幅に調整する中で、エネルギーセクターが強い相対的優位性を持っていることを示しています。WTI原油価格の現在の水準と、**SLB**のMarket Microstructure、**COP**のEarnings Intelligence、**E**のShort Interest & Lending Intelligenceといった特定の分析次元からの明確な強気シグナルが、セクターの今後の上昇を支えるでしょう。

投資家は、主要な個別銘柄における継続的な買い圧力と取引量の推移を監視し、マクロ環境における原油価格と金利の変動に注意を払うことが推奨されます。現在のデータは、エネルギーセクターが短期から中期的に市場全体のパフォーマンスを上回る可能性を示唆しています。

*Source: [エネルギーセクターが市場の逆風に逆らい強気姿勢を維持：SLBがリード](https://haruspex.guru/insights/sector-analysis-2026-08-31-energy-ja)*

An average score of 62.0 for 45 stocks, with 42 signaling bullish moves, suggests continued upside. Investors should monitor individual stock dimensions like Market Microstructure and Earnings Intelligence for specific entry points.

On Monday, August 31, 2026 ET, the broader market experienced a slight pullback, with the S&P 500 closing at 7680.65, down 0.40% [data: 2026-08-31 close]. However, the Energy sector demonstrated notable resilience, with Haruspex analysis indicating persistent bullish directional pressure.

## Energy Sector Maintains Bullish Edge

The Energy sector, comprising 45 stocks, posted an average Haruspex score of 62.0 out of 100, indicating a clear bullish trading edge overall. A significant 42 stocks within the sector registered bullish signals, with no stocks showing bearish pressure, reinforcing a strong positive sentiment.

This widespread bullish sentiment contrasts with the marginal dip in the S&P 500, suggesting that sector-specific tailwinds are at play. Harupsex analysis points to underlying market dynamics that are favoring energy-related assets, even as general market cautiousness prevails.

### Top Energy Stocks Showing Strong Signals

Several individual stocks within the Energy sector demonstrated particularly strong directional pressure:

- **SLB** (Schlumberger Limited) registered a strong bullish pressure score of 72.0, increasing by 2.0 points. This upward directional signal was primarily driven by its Market Microstructure dimension, which detected 85% buying pressure on 180% above-average volume. Investors should observe for sustained buying pressure and follow-through volume.
- **FRO** (Frontline plc) maintained a bullish pressure score of 69.0. Its Competitive Intelligence dimension highlighted its position as a leading player, ranking #1 by market cap among five peers in the Oil & Gas Midstream industry. This competitive edge contributes to its sustained positive market behavior.
- **ET** (Energy Transfer LP) saw its score rise by 3.0 points to 69.0, indicating bullish pressure. The analysis for **ET** indicated activity through its Algorithmic sentiment analysis, although specific AI insights were unavailable for deeper characterization.
- **XOM** (Exxon Mobil Corporation) experienced a significant 9.0-point surge, bringing its score to 68.0, signaling bullish pressure. While its Market Microstructure dimension showed balanced order flow and excellent liquidity, the notable score increase suggests broader positive market activity. Monitoring volume and order flow is key.
- **COP** (ConocoPhillips) climbed 6.0 points to a score of 68.0, indicating bullish pressure. This move was primarily supported by its Earnings Intelligence dimension, with the company beating EPS estimates by 14.1% and achieving 70.5% YoY EPS growth. Continued strong earnings performance will be vital for this trend.
- **EOG** (EOG Resources Inc.) also showed bullish pressure with a score of 67.0, up 3.0 points. Its Earnings Intelligence dimension registered a strong signal as **EOG** beat EPS estimates by 10.8%. This positive earnings performance is a critical factor driving market interest.
- **E** (Eni S.p.A.) saw a 3.0-point increase to 67.0, signaling bullish pressure. This was heavily influenced by its Short Interest & Lending Intelligence dimension, with a mere 0.1% of its float shorted, representing a 99.9% reduction from the prior month. This suggests potential short covering activity.
- **CVX** (Chevron Corporation) rose 6.0 points to a score of 66.0, showing bullish pressure. The Earnings Intelligence dimension was a key contributor, with **CVX** beating EPS estimates by 8.4% and maintaining a 100% beat rate over the last four quarters.

### What is driving the consistent bullish pressure in Energy?

The widespread bullish pressure in the Energy sector stems from a combination of favorable macro conditions and robust company-specific performance detected by Haruspex's various dimensions. Stable WTI Crude Oil prices above the $85 mark provide a supportive revenue environment, while specific dimensions like Earnings Intelligence, Market Microstructure, Competitive Intelligence, and Short Interest & Lending Intelligence frequently show positive signals for individual stocks.

## Sector Drivers

Haruspex analysis indicates that several key dimensions are collectively driving the bullish pressure across the Energy sector. Earnings Intelligence is a significant factor, with companies like **COP**, **EOG**, and **CVX** reporting strong beats. Market Microstructure, as seen with **SLB**, points to active buying pressure. Additionally, Competitive Intelligence (e.g., **FRO**) and shifts in Short Interest & Lending Intelligence (e.g., **E**) are contributing to the positive directional signals.

## Regime Thresholds

The current bullish thesis for the Energy sector is strongly tied to the stability of WTI Crude Oil prices. As long as WTI Crude remains above $80, the sector is likely to sustain its current positive momentum. A move above $90 for WTI would amplify bullish pressure, potentially leading to further upside. Conversely, a sustained break below $75 for WTI could erode the current market sentiment and introduce bearish pressure.

The 10Y Treasury Yield also plays a role. While currently at 4.76% [data: 2026-08-31 close], a significant and sustained increase above 5.0% could increase capital costs for energy projects, tempering some bullish outlooks. The DXY at 99.47 [data: 2026-08-31 close] also bears watching, as a stronger dollar can impact global oil demand and pricing.

## Risk Factors

Despite the strong bullish signals, several risks could impact the Energy sector's trajectory. Geopolitical instability in key oil-producing regions could disrupt supply chains or impact global demand, leading to price volatility. A material slowdown in global economic growth could reduce demand for energy, thereby putting downward pressure on crude oil prices and energy stock revenues.

Furthermore, evolving regulatory landscapes regarding carbon emissions or new energy transition policies could introduce operational challenges and capital expenditure requirements for traditional energy companies. These factors require constant monitoring to assess their potential impact on sector performance.

## What Would Change This View

The current bullish view for the Energy sector would face invalidation under specific, measurable conditions. If WTI Crude Oil were to close below $78 for a full week on significant volume, it would signal a potential breakdown in price support. A broad market correction in the S&P 500, particularly a sustained move below 7,500 that leads to widespread deleveraging across equity markets, could also exert significant downward pressure on energy stocks regardless of sector-specific fundamentals.

Additionally, a rapid and sustained increase in the 10Y Treasury Yield above 5.0% could lead to a repricing of future cash flows and increased borrowing costs, potentially dampening investor enthusiasm for capital-intensive energy projects.

## Outlook

Haruspex analysis indicates that the Energy sector is well-positioned to maintain its bullish market pressure in the near term, provided macro conditions, particularly WTI Crude Oil prices, remain supportive. The widespread positive signals across various dimensions for individual stocks suggest that underlying market dynamics favor continued upside. Investors should focus on companies demonstrating consistent earnings strength and favorable Market Microstructure signals, while closely monitoring key macro thresholds for any shifts in the broader environment.

## Cross-Theme Connections

The simultaneous strength of both the Energy & Commodities and Interest Rate Pressure themes indicates a complex interplay of macro forces. Rising oil prices often contribute to broader inflationary pressures, which in turn can prompt central banks to maintain higher interest rates or signals further rate hikes.

This dynamic creates a feedback loop where commodity inflation and monetary policy reinforce each other's market impact.

For a company like **DE**, this means navigating both higher input costs from commodities and potentially higher borrowing costs. However, the strong Institutional Ownership Intelligence suggests that large investors perceive **DE** as resilient or benefiting from specific industry tailwinds that offset these pressures.

Meanwhile, for companies like **SNAP**, while less directly tied to commodities, the inflationary environment can affect consumer spending and advertising budgets.

Higher interest rates can also influence investor appetite for growth stocks. The bullish Algorithmic sentiment analysis for **SNAP** suggests that despite these headwinds, positive market perception is strong.

### How do rising oil prices and bond yields create market pressure?

Rising oil prices directly affect the cost of goods and transportation, contributing to inflation. This inflationary pressure often leads central banks to increase interest rates to cool the economy.

Higher bond yields, reflecting these rising rates, make fixed-income investments more attractive relative to equities, especially growth stocks. They also increase the cost of capital for businesses, impacting profitability and investment decisions.

This dual pressure creates a dynamic where commodity-driven inflation meets monetary tightening, shaping investment flows and equity performance.

Looking ahead, the market will likely continue to digest macro indicators, particularly any further movements in bond yields and energy prices. Haruspex will be monitoring for continuation in the bullish trends seen in **DDS** and **SCHW**, looking for further confirmation in their underlying dimension scores. ## Haruspexが検出した内容

Haruspexの分析は、従来の単一要因スクリーンでは見逃されがちな複数の次元を横断して市場の方向性圧力を検出します。今日の市場では、特にマイクロストラクチャル次元とショートインタレスト次元が多数の銘柄で強気のシグナルを生成しました。

マイクロストラクチャル次元は、実際の注文フロー、出来高、およびスプレッドを分析することで、買い手と売り手の間のバランスの変化を識別します。例えば、**SCHW**ではマイクロストラクチャル次元が79に上昇し、75%の買いフローと170%の平均出来高を検出。これは、機関投資家による強い蓄積圧力を示唆するものです。

ショートインタレスト次元は、空売り残高の変化と空売りのカバー状況を監視します。**BIO**（Bio-Rad Laboratories, Inc.）では、ショートインタレスト次元が66に上昇し、浮動株の0.0%が空売りされており、前月比100.0%減少しています。これは、ショートカバーが進行中である可能性を示唆し、追加的な買い圧力を生み出します。

### マイクロストラクチャルデータはなぜ重要なのか？

マイクロストラクチャルデータは、市場のリアルタイムの注文フローと約定動向を反映するため重要です。これは、しばしば広範なテクニカルな動きに先行して、市場参加者の実際の行動を捉えます。単純な出来高スクリーンでは見えない機関投資家の買い圧力や売り圧力を検出し、より詳細な取引エッジを提供します。

### レジーム閾値

現在の広範な市場における強気シグナルの妥当性は、特定のマクロ条件に左右されます。S&P 500が7600ポイントを上回って推移している間、市場全体の強気センチメントは継続すると考えられます。VIXが18を下回る水準を維持することも、市場の安定性を示す重要な指標です。

また、10年物米国債利回りが4.80%を超えない限り、成長株への圧力が過度に高まることはないと分析されています。WTI原油が90ドルを下回っている状況は、インフレ圧力の緩和に貢献し、企業の収益性を支援する要因となります。

## リスク要因

- **金利上昇の再燃**: 10年物米国債利回りが予期せず上昇した場合、特に成長志向の銘柄や金利敏感なセクターに圧力がかかる可能性があります。
- **エネルギー価格の変動**: WTI原油価格が再び大幅に上昇し、例えば90ドルを超える水準で定着した場合、製造業や輸送業のコスト増につながり、広範なインフレ圧力を再燃させる恐れがあります。
- **地政学的イベント**: 予期せぬ地政学的緊張や紛争の激化は、投資家のリスク回避行動を促し、市場全体のセンチメントを急速に悪化させる可能性があります。

## この見方を変える条件

現在の強気な市場圧力に関する見方は、以下の具体的な条件が満たされた場合に変化する可能性があります。

- S&P 500が7500ポイントを下回り、その水準で終値を迎える週がフルで観察された場合、広範な市場の強気圧力のテーゼは延期される可能性があります。
- VIXが18を上回り、2日連続で終値をつけた場合、市場の不確実性とボラティリティが増大し、投資家のリスク志向が減退するでしょう。
- 主要な金融銘柄（例：**SCHW**、**UBS**、**NMR**）において、マイクロストラクチャル次元で継続的かつ顕著な売り圧力が検出された場合、セクター全体の市場圧力見通しが下方修正されます。

## 今後の見通し

Haruspexの分析に基づくと、広範な市場センチメントは短期的には引き続き強気で推移すると予測されます。特に、マイクロストラクチャル次元とショートインタレスト次元が示す個別銘柄レベルの買い圧力が、この傾向を牽引するでしょう。

投資家は、これらの強力なシグナルを示す銘柄に注目し、買いフローの継続と出来高の推移を監視する必要があります。同時に、金利や原油価格といったマクロ指標の動きを注意深く観察し、市場のレジーム変化の兆候を見逃さないことが重要です。

## Interest Rate Pressure Dominates Market Direction

The prevailing market theme today was significant Interest Rate Pressure, registering a strength of 65 out of 100. This pressure stems directly from the 10Y Treasury Yield holding at 4.72% [data: 2026-08-29 close], notably above the 4.5% threshold. This elevation creates a challenging environment for many sectors, particularly those reliant on future earnings growth and accessible capital.

Technology and growth stocks often face headwinds when interest rates climb, as higher discount rates reduce the present value of future cash flows. This macro signal contributed to shifts in directional pressure for several key stocks.

- **Salesforce Inc.** (**CRM**) saw its market pressure score move to 55.0, a change of -16.0, reflecting a neutral outlook. Despite the broader rate pressure, the `short_interest_lending_intelligence` dimension detected bullish pressure. This is due to 3.7% of its float being shorted, a 99.9% decrease from the prior month, suggesting significant short covering activity.
- **Halliburton Company** (**HAL**) registered a score of 56.0, a change of -14.0, maintaining a bullish outlook. The `institutional_ownership_intelligence` dimension indicates strong bullish pressure, with 90.5% institutional ownership across 1,633 institutions and net institutional buying detected. This suggests resilience against macro headwinds.
- **Lucid Group Inc.** (**LCID**) recorded a score of 43.0, a change of -13.0, leading to a bearish outlook. Its `institutional_ownership_intelligence` dimension shows a neutral-to-mixed signal, despite 68.2% institutional ownership. Harupex analysis notes net institutional buying, but also highlights high concentration in top holders, posing volatility risk in a high-rate environment.

This decline suggests a potential shift in investor sentiment regarding safe-haven assets, or a repricing of inflation expectations and risk.

A downturn in gold can sometimes coincide with a perception of decreased systemic risk or an increased opportunity cost compared to rising bond yields. This can affect how investors allocate capital across various asset classes, influencing market pressure signals for individual stocks.

- **Salesforce Inc.** (**CRM**) felt the combined impact, with its market pressure score at 55.0. While the `short_interest_lending_intelligence` dimension provided a specific bullish counter-signal, the overall score movement reflects the broader market's uncertainty driven by both rates and gold's performance.

## Cross-Theme Connections: Navigating Competing Forces

The interplay between rising interest rates and a falling gold price creates a complex market narrative. Typically, higher yields can make non-yielding assets like gold less attractive, as the opportunity cost of holding gold increases. Today's data aligns with this, showing both the 10Y yield rising and gold prices falling.

This dynamic impacts different stock types in varied ways. Growth-oriented companies, like **CRM** and especially **LCID**, often see their valuations pressured by higher discount rates. However, unique company-specific signals, such as the `short_interest_lending_intelligence` in **CRM**, can create localized bullish counter-currents.

Conversely, sectors perceived as inflation hedges or having strong institutional backing, such as parts of the Energy sector where **HAL** resides, may show resilience. The Energy sector, in general, displayed the highest average score (60.8) and 35 bullish stocks out of 38, suggesting a defensive or value-driven allocation trend despite broader macro concerns.

### How are rising yields impacting tech and growth stocks?

Rising interest rates directly increase the cost of capital and future earnings are discounted at a higher rate, which particularly affects companies with substantial growth expectations in the distant future. For a tech company like **CRM**, this macro headwind can dampen overall market pressure, despite specific bullish indicators like short covering.

For high-growth, capital-intensive companies like **LCID**, the impact is even more pronounced. The bearish pressure on **LCID**, despite some institutional buying, highlights how the perceived risk and cost of future growth are amplified in a higher-rate environment. The detected concentration risk in `institutional_ownership_intelligence` can exacerbate this volatility.

## Regime Thresholds: Monitoring Key Market Inflection Points

Understanding specific levels where market dynamics can shift is crucial. For the Interest Rate Pressure theme, as long as the 10Y Treasury Yield remains consistently above 4.5%, the pressure on growth-oriented sectors and companies with high debt loads is expected to persist, influencing directional signals.

A sustained close of gold prices below $4500 could indicate diminishing safe-haven demand or a stronger risk-on sentiment in the coming days. This shift may trigger further capital reallocation away from traditional safe assets. Additionally, WTI Crude Oil above $80, currently at $83.40 [data: 2026-08-29 close], continues to present inflationary pressures, affecting consumer spending and corporate margins across various sectors.

## What Would Change This View

Several specific conditions could alter the current market outlook regarding interest rate and gold dynamics:

- **For Interest Rate Pressure:** A decisive and sustained reversal in the 10Y Treasury Yield, closing below 4.5% for three consecutive trading days, would signal easing monetary concerns and potentially alleviate pressure on growth stocks.
- **For Interest Rate Pressure:** A clear dovish pivot from central bank communications, explicitly signaling future rate cuts or a pause in tightening, would likely shift market expectations and investor behavior.
- **For Gold & Safe Haven:** Gold prices recovering and holding above $4600 for a full trading week, particularly if accompanied by increased geopolitical uncertainty, would indicate renewed safe-haven demand and a potential re-evaluation of risk.
- **For Gold & Safe Haven:** A significant and persistent increase in market volatility (e.g., VIX above 20), especially if not tied to specific geopolitical events, could also drive investors back to gold as a traditional hedge.

## Outlook: Navigating Macro Headwinds and Selective Resilience

The market on August 29, 2026, presented a landscape shaped by significant macro headwinds. Rising interest rates, evidenced by the 10Y Treasury Yield above 4.7%, created a challenging environment, particularly for growth-focused sectors. Concurrently, a notable decline in gold prices suggests a shift in how investors are positioning for safety or inflation, potentially signaling a move away from traditional hedges.

Haruspex analysis indicates that while these macro forces are broad, stock-specific dynamics remain critical. **CRM** experienced a net decrease in directional pressure, but its `short_interest_lending_intelligence` pointed to a localized bullish signal from short covering. **HAL** demonstrated institutional resilience, bolstered by strong `institutional_ownership_intelligence` despite the challenging macro backdrop.

Conversely, **LCID** faced increased bearish pressure, underscoring the vulnerability of high-growth, capital-intensive companies to elevated interest rates and broad market uncertainty. The Energy sector stands out with the highest average score (60.8), suggesting selective resilience. Investors should continue to monitor yield trajectories and gold's future price action closely, alongside specific company-level dimensions, to discern emerging opportunities and risks.

## 2026年8月28日：規制圧力高まる中、市場は緩やかな強気トレンドを維持

**Category:** Market Trends | **Published:** 2026-08-28 | **Language:** ja

> Haruspexデータは、市場全体で57.5の平均スコアと62%の強気シグナルを検出。規制圧力とショート関心の高まりが注目され、特定セクターの動向に影響を与えています。強気トレンドは維持される見込みですが、特定の弱点にも注意が必要です。

### Stock Scores

| Symbol | Company | Score | Change | Outlook |
|--------|---------|-------|--------|---------|
| A | Agilent Technologies, Inc. | 73.0 | +3.0 | bullish |
| DT | Dynatrace, Inc. | 73.0 | +1.0 | bullish |

**TL;DR:** Haruspexデータは、2026年8月28日現在、市場全体で62%の強気シグナルと平均57.5のスコアを示しています。規制圧力とショート関心の高まりが顕著で、特定銘柄に強い市場圧力を生み出しています。投資家は、これらのディメンション動向が示唆するセクターと銘柄に注目すべきです。

2026年8月28日 ET、市場は様々なディメンションシグナルの中で取引を終えました。Haruspex分析によると、調査対象の712銘柄全体で、市場の平均スコアは57.5を記録しました。全体の62%の銘柄が強気の方向性を示しています。

このデータは、市場に緩やかな強気圧力が広範に存在することを示唆しています。しかし、詳細なディメンション分析は、特定の領域で異なる強度の市場圧力が働いていることを明らかにしています。

## マクロ環境モニター

現在のマクロ経済指標は、市場の背景にある条件を示しています。

| 指標 | 現在値 | 主要水準 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| S&P 500 | 7724.34 (-0.09%) [data: 2026-08-28 close] | 抵抗線 7800 |
| VIX | 14.27 [data: 2026-08-28 close] | 恐怖指数の中立 |
| 米国債10年利回り | 4.70% [data: 2026-08-28 close] | 中立レンジ |
| WTI原油 | $83.03 [data: 2026-08-28 close] | 高値圏 |
| 米ドル指数 (DXY) | 99.53 [data: 2026-08-28 close] | 中立レンジ |

S&P 500はわずかな下落で終えましたが、全体的な市場のボラティリティはVIXが14.27と低い水準を維持しています。これは、投資家の間に広範な不確実性が少ないことを示唆します。米国債10年利回りは4.70%で推移しています。

WTI原油は83.03ドルと高値圏にあり、一部のセクターではコスト圧力につながる可能性があります。DXYは99.53で推移しており、ドルの相対的な安定性を示しています。

## データが示すもの

Haruspex分析は、いくつかの主要なディメンションにおける市場圧力の顕著な変化を特定しました。特に、規制とショート・インタレストのディメンションが強いシグナルを発しています。

ショート・インタレスト・ディメンションは平均62.1のスコアを示しており、依然として最も強い強気圧力を検出しています。これは、市場がショートポジションに対して強い方向性の優位性を感じていることを意味します。この圧力は、**DT**、**BIO**、**NOW**といった銘柄で特に顕著です。

規制ディメンションは平均61.7のスコアを記録し、週次で+1.7ポイント上昇しました。これは、規制当局の活動や政策変更が市場に与える影響が強まっていることを示唆します。**V**、**MA**、**AAPL**といった大手企業がこの規制圧力のトップ銘柄として挙げられています。

機関投資家ディメンションは平均61.2のスコアで、これらの銘柄に対する機関投資家の関心が高いことを示しています。**CR**、**SHOP**、**MXL**がこの影響を受けています。センチメント・ディメンションも平均60.4で強気の圧力を示しており、**AVGO**、**TTWO**、**WULF**で前向きな市場心理が見られます。

アーニングス・ディメンションは平均58.4で、今後の決算発表への期待が一部の銘柄に影響を与えていることを示唆します。**ARM**、**PIPR**、**TTMI**などがその例です。これらのディメンションにおける強気圧力は、個別の銘柄動向を理解する上で重要です。

### 規制圧力の高まりは何を示唆しているのか？

規制ディメンションのスコア上昇は、規制当局からの監視強化や業界に影響を与える新たな政策の可能性を示唆します。特に**V**、**MA**、**AAPL**といった銘柄が上位に挙がっていることは、大規模なテクノロジー企業や金融サービス企業がより多くの精査に直面していることを意味します。

これは、これらの企業の事業運営や収益モデルに潜在的な影響を与える可能性があります。投資家は、これらの企業が発表する規制関連のニュースや、市場でのその後の反応を注意深く監視する必要があります。

## リスク要因

市場全体に強気シグナルが広がる一方で、いくつかのディメンションは異なる市場圧力を示しています。インサイダー取引ディメンションは平均34.7のスコアで、強い弱気圧力を検出しています。これは、インサイダーが積極的に売却していることを示唆しています。

特に**TSM**、**TSLA**、**AMZN**といった主要銘柄でこのインサイダーの売りが見られます。これは、これらの企業内部で将来の見通しに対する警戒感があることを示唆する可能性があります。広範な市場の強気シグナルと矛盾するこの動きは、注意を払う必要があります。

コンセントレーション・リスク・ディメンションは平均45.2のスコアであり、方向性の優位性は明確ではありません。しかし、**UNH**、**TSM**、**BRK.B**といった銘柄で高いスコアが見られることは、市場が少数の大手企業に過度に依存している可能性を示しています。これは、これらの企業に問題が発生した場合に市場全体に波及するリスクを提起します。

ESGディメンションは平均0.0のスコアであり、この領域からの市場圧力は検出されていません。これは、ESG要因が現在の市場の方向性を大きく左右する要因ではないことを示しています。

## この見方を変えるものは何か

現在の緩やかな強気トレンドに対する見方は、特定の市場条件によって変化する可能性があります。

- S&P 500が7500を下回って週を終え、その週の出来高が平均を上回る場合、広範な強気圧力のテーゼは後退します。
- VIXが20を超え、数日間その水準を維持する場合、市場のボラティリティの急増が現在の強気見通しを無効にする可能性があります。
- 米国債10年利回りが5.00%を超えて持続的に上昇する場合、金利感応度の高いセクターに強い下押し圧力がかかり、市場全体のムードを変えるでしょう。

## アウトルック

Haruspexデータは、市場に緩やかな強気圧力が存在する中で、特定のディメンションがより強い方向性を示していることを明らかにしています。規制当局の監視強化とショート・インタレストの強気シグナルは、今後数週間の市場動向を形成する主要な要因となるでしょう。

投資家は、規制関連のニュースと、**V**、**MA**、**AAPL**などの主要銘柄への影響を監視すべきです。また、**DT**、**BIO**、**NOW**などのショート・インタレストが高い銘柄には、潜在的な短期的な動きが発生する可能性があります。インサイダー取引に見られる弱気シグナルも、注意深く評価されるべき重要なリスク要因です。

全体として、市場は安定したマクロ環境の中で、特定のセクターや銘柄に焦点を当てた動きを続けると予想されます。データの詳細な分析は、投資家がこの変化する市場環境をナビゲートする上で不可欠なツールとなります。

*Source: [2026年8月28日：規制圧力高まる中、市場は緩やかな強気トレンドを維持](https://haruspex.guru/insights/market-trends-2026-08-28-ja)*

## August 28, 2026: Short-Interest & Regulatory Drive Bullish Market Pressure

**Category:** Market Trends | **Published:** 2026-08-28 | **Language:** en

> Haruspex data for August 28, 2026 shows 62% of stocks exhibiting bullish pressure, led by strong Short-Interest and rising Regulatory signals. Focus on specific

### Stock Scores

| Symbol | Company | Score | Change | Outlook |
|--------|---------|-------|--------|---------|
| A | Agilent Technologies, Inc. | 73.0 | +3.0 | bullish |
| DT | Dynatrace, Inc. | 73.0 | +1.0 | bullish |
